focus_tree = {
	id = tex_focus
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			tag = TEX
		}
	}
	default = no
	continuous_focus_position = { x = 500 y = 2000 }	
### Phillip the First Path ###
	focus = {# The New Gunpowder Plot
		id = TEX_new_gunpowder_plot
		icon = GFX_goal_TEX_focus_Gunpowder_plot
		cost = 5
		available = {
			has_country_flag = TEX_allowed_catholic
			has_government = national_populist
				NOT = { 
					TEX_Joe_McWilliams = {
						is_country_leader = yes
					}	
					has_country_flag = TEX_smith_victory
					has_country_flag = TEX_coughlin_victory
					has_country_flag = CP_Victory
				}
		}
		bypass = {
			has_country_flag = TEX_allowed_catholic
			has_government = national_populist
			NOT = { 
					TEX_Joe_McWilliams = {
						is_country_leader = yes
					}	
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 6 }
		x = -7
		y = 0
		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus TEX_new_gunpowder_plot"
			custom_effect_tooltip = unlock_focuses
			remove_ideas = TEX_prohibition_bill
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = national_populist
				long_name = TEX_national_populist_catholic_party_long
				name = TEX_national_populist_catholic_party
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = national_populist
				popularity = 0.1
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = TEX_cath_power }
		}
	}
